11 Cadre review of CHS
Provision has been made in the proposed Revised
CHS Rules which has got concurrence of D/o
Personnel and Training for concurrence. The
same will be sent to UPSC.
The proposal has been referred to DGHS for their
comments.
The proposal was referred to 6
th Pay Commission.
Even though NPA from 25% to 40% was not
agreed to by 6
th CPC, the Ministry has
recommended the proposal with slight modification
i.e. enhancement of NPA from 25% to 30% to
COS constituted by the Government to look into
the grievances/demands of various Associations/
Organisations.
The proposal has been referred to 6
th Pay
Commission and the same has been agreed to.
D/o Personnel and Training had informed that
Amendment in Consolidated Instructions on
Foreign Assignment of Indian Experts is under
process and this Ministry’s suggestions have
been noted down and will be considered
while finalizing the Consolidated Instructions.
Consolidated instructions on foreign assignment
of Indian Experts issued by DOPT vide O.M. No.
AB-14017/2/07-Estt.(RR) dated 29.2.2008 does
not include these suggestions and DOPT has been
requested to re-consider the matter for inclusion
of this Ministry’s proposal in the terms and
conditions for foreign assignment.
A Research Incentive Scheme has been prepared
and the same is being finalized.
Orders in this regard has been issued by IC
Division vide  Order No. L.21020/85/2000-IC
dated 26.11.2007.
The process of Cadre review has already been
initiated. A meeting of the participating units of CHS
was held on 12.3.2007 under the Chairmanship of
Secretary (H&FW). The information received from
various units were compiled and the proposals were
discussed with each unit Head in a series of
meetings taken by JS(VC). Subsequently DGHS
held the meeting on 16.6.2008 and decided to
collect some additional information.

ANNEXURE-IV
Ministry of Finance
Department of Expenditure
E.II (A) Branch
Subject: Dispensation under National Rural Health Mission.
Ministry of Health and Family Welfare may refer to their proposal in D.O. letter No. L.1335/
Secy (HFW)/2007 dated 21.11.2007 on the subject.
2. The proposal has been examined in this Department. As regards Proposal-I seeking
dispensation under Rule 209 (6) (iii) of the GFRs, the following has been agreed to:—
(i) By relaxing conditions under Rule 209 (6) (iii) for release of recurring grants, unspent
balances  of 2006-07 may not be taken into account for release to be made during
the financial year 2007-08. This relaxation, however, will be limited till 31.12.2007
and only with respect to funds under the Mission Flexi Pool of NRHM.
(ii) 75% of recurring grants may be released during 2007-08 based on provisional
certificates without considering unspent balances of FY 2006-07, while remaining
amount may be released as per provisions of GFRs, 2005
(iii) Further, only unspent balances as in (ii), which are explicitly acknowledges by
Statement of Expenditure duly signed by authorized persons shall be recognized for
the purpose. Where no such accounts have been rendered, this relaxation will not be
applicable.
(iv) A report should be furnished to this Department immediately to the effect that all UCs
for releases made during 2005-06 have been obtained as per the format and
requirement of GFRs.
(v) A review of the position will be carried out in the last quarter of the current financial
year (2007-08).
(vi) It is reiterated that the above dispensation by relaxing the provisions of GFRs shall
be limited for release of funds under Mission Flexi-Pool of NRHM programme only.
3. The Proposal-II allowing National Programme Coordination Committee (NPCC) to approve
financial allocations for the State Sector Programme Implementation Programmes (PIPs) for more
than the BE of the concerned financial year has not been agreed to.
4. This issues with the approval of Secretary (Expenditure).
Sd/-
(Rubina Ali)
Under Secretary-E.II(A)
AS&FA (Health)
Ministry of Finance D/o Expr. I.D. No.8/4 E.II (A)/05, dated 20.12.2007

ANNEXURE-VI
INFORMATION ON THE PERFORMANCE OF NGO
Report 1
➢ Name of the District where evaluation was conducted – East Khasi Hills, Meghalaya
➢ Internal Evaluation conducted during the period – 9th – 12th August, 2007
➢ Name of 1 NGO selected in that district – World Vision of India
➢ Date of signing the MOU with the DTCS – lst March, 2004
➢ Scheme under which the NGO is involved – Scheme I and II
➢ Population covered by the NGO – 150000
➢ Activities performed by the NGO – The NGO sensitizes and train volunteers,
disseminate information, provide counseling to patients and families, sensitize, orient
and advocate with key groups, and, retrieve defaulters in their area.
NGOs also play a role in formulating, developing, and revising information, education and
communication (IEC) materials which are appropriate for the local context. The role of the
NGO is also to identify, train, and supervise volunteers who are providing DOT. The NGO
ensures that treatment and Records ( e.g. Tuberculosis Treatment Card) are maintained
strictly as per RNTCP policy.
➢ Funds released to the NGO in the financial year 2006-2007 – Rs.4000/-
➢ Performance of the NGO in the year 2006-2007 with respect to district performance-
CAT I: 2 cases; CAT II: 3 cases; CAT III: 2 cases
➢ Has the NOO submitted Utilisation certificate and Audit report? Yes
Report 2
➢ Name of the District where evaluation was conducted and when?
Pathanamthitta, 21-24th February, 2007
➢ Name of 1 NGO selected in that district?
SLET (Sahyadri Leprosy Eradication Trust), Ranni, Pathanamthitta
➢ Date of signing the MOU with the DTCS.
14/2/2005, was working without any signed schemes since 2003, scheme IV
signed in 2005, being renewed every year.
237

238
➢ Scheme under which the NGO is involved - Scheme IV
➢ Population covered by the NGO - 1 Lakh
➢ Activities performed by the NGO -
The NGO provides AFB microscopy and TB treatment services free of charge. The
NGO is responsible for ensuring the treatment or referral of all patients found to
have a positive AFB smear, and for ensuring follow-up treatment and sputum
examinations for all patients placed on treatment. The NGO ensures referral for
treatment of patients found to be smear-positive but who live outside the NGO’s
catchment area. In the case of patients with chest symptoms who are found to have
negative AFB smears or are suspected to have other forms of tuberculosis, the
NGO will either evaluate the patient as per RNTCP policy, or will refer the patient
to an identified referral centre for such evaluation. The NGO also provides a
trained laboratory technician and a qualified Medical Officer (MO) trained in the
RNTCP.
➢ Funds released to the NGO in the financial year 2006-2007.
Rs 50,000/-
➢ Performance of the NGO in the year 2006-2007 with respect to district performance.
Very good case detection and case holding. There is consistent good
performance for the last 4 years.
➢ Has the NGO submitted Utilisation certificate and Audit report?
YES
Report 3
➢ Name of the district:  South 24 Parganas.(evaluation done in 10-14th march, 2008)
➢ Name of the NGO: Southern health Improvement society (SHIS)
➢ Date of signing MOU with DTCS:  6th July 2003
➢ Scheme under which the NGO is involved:  Scheme-V (Running 3 TUs in the
riverine belts of Sunderbans)
➢ Population covered by the NGO  – 15 Lakhs
➢ Activities performed by the NGO –
The NGO ensures full services for microscopy, treatment, direct observation, defaulter
retrieval, recording and registration, supervision, etc. and ensure all programme
implementation responsibilities. The NGO coordinates closely with all public and other health
facilities in the area. The NGO maintains RNTCP records and submits quarterly reports to
the District TB Officer in the prescribed manner and in a timely fashion.

239
➢ Funds released in the FY 2006-07 to the NGO:  Rs.10,20,000 (Rupees Ten lakhs and
twenty thousand only)
➢ Performance of the NGO in FY 2006-07 in respect to district performance:
Average ACDR of the 3 TUs is 132, Average NSP case detection percentage of 3 TUs
is 71 %, average Cure rates are 94%. All the performance indicators are better than
the overall district performances.
➢ Has the NGO submitted UC and audit report?
Yes through the district in December , 2007.
